1023:     private int getSwampDifficult(int ui) {
1024:         if (this.T_BoP) {
1025:             if (this.IsEasySwamp(ui)) {
1026:                 return 3;
1027:             }
1028:             if (this.IsNormalSwamp(ui)) {
1029:                 return 2;
1030:             }
1031:             if (this.IsDifficultSwamp(ui)) {
1032:                 return 1;
1033:             }
1034:             return 0;
1035:         }
1036:         return 0;
1037:     }

1039:     private void generateClearWater(int xx, int yy, int zz, int rad, World world, Random random) {
1040:         double rad_cof = Math.max((double)rad / 10.0, 1.0);
1041:         double rax = 1.0;
1042:         double raz = 1.0;
1043:         for (int j = 0; j <= rad / 2; ++j) {
1044:             for (int i = 0; i <= rad; ++i) {
1045:                 for (int e = 0; e <= rad; ++e) {
1046:                     int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
1047:                     int posy = yy - j;
1048:                     int posz = zz + e - rad / 2;
1049:                     double dis = Math.sqrt(Math.pow(xx - posx, 2.0) + Math.pow(yy - posy, 2.0) + Math.pow(zz - posz, 2.0));
1050:                     if (!(dis < (double)rad / 2.0 / (1.0 + Math.abs(rax - raz) / 3.0) + (random.nextDouble() * rad_cof - rad_cof / 2.0)) || this.validWaterMaterial(posx, posy + 1, posz, world) || !this.validWaterMaterial(posx, posy, posz, world)) continue;
1051:                     world.func_147468_f(posx, posy, posz);
1052:                 }
1053:             }
1054:         }

2440:     private void generateCaveWebbing(int xx, int yy, int zz, int rad, World world, Random random) {
2441:         int posz;
2442:         int posy;
2443:         int posx;
2444:         int e;

2448:         double rax = random.nextDouble() + 0.5;
2449:         double raz = 1.0 - rax + 0.5 + 0.5;
2450:         for (i = 0; i <= rad; ++i) {
2451:             for (j = 0; j <= rad / 2; ++j) {

2454:                     posy = yy - j;
2455:                     posz = zz + e - rad / 2;

2457:                     if (!(dis < (double)rad / 2.0 / (1.0 + Math.abs(rax - raz) / 3.0) + (random.nextDouble() * rad_cof - rad_cof / 2.0))) continue;

2460:                         if (this.validCaveMaterial(posx, posy + 2, posz, world) && !this.validWaterMaterial(posx, posy + 3, posz, world)) {

2467:                                         world.func_147468_f(posx, posy + 5, posz);
2468:                                     }
2469:                                 }
2470:                             }
2471:                         }

2477:             }

2480:             for (j = 0; j <= rad / 2 + 2; ++j) {
2481:                 for (e = 0; e <= rad; ++e) {
2482:                     posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
2483:                     posy = yy - j;
2484:                     posz = zz + e - rad / 2;
2485:                     if (world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz) == MFQM.DenseWebBlock || this.validGroundMaterial(posx, posy, posz, world) || world.func_147439_a(posx - 1, posy, posz) != MFQM.DenseWebBlock && world.func_147439_a(posx + 1, posy, posz) != MFQM.DenseWebBlock && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy + 1, posz) != MFQM.DenseWebBlock && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz - 1) != MFQM.DenseWebBlock && world.func_147439_a(posx, posy, posz + 1) != MFQM.DenseWebBlock) continue;

2734:     private void generateWater(int xx, int yy, int zz, int rad, World world, Random random, int wty) {
2735:         int posz;
2736:         int posy;
2737:         int posx;
2738:         int e;
2739:         int j;
2740:         int i;
2741:         boolean peat = false;
2742:         Block sblock = this.soilBlock(xx, zz, world);
2743:         Block fblock = this.fillerBlock(xx, zz, world);
2744:         if (random.nextInt(10) == 0) {
2745:             peat = true;
2746:         }
2747:         double rad_cof = Math.max((double)rad / 10.0, 1.0);
2748:         double rax = random.nextDouble() + 0.5;
2749:         double raz = 1.0 - rax + 0.5 + 0.5;
2750:         for (i = 0; i <= rad; ++i) {
2751:             for (j = 0; j <= rad / 2; ++j) {
2752:                 for (e = 0; e <= rad; ++e) {
2753:                     posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
2754:                     posy = yy - j;

3692:     private void ClearArea(int xx, int yy, int zz, int rad, World world, Random random) {
3693:         double rad_cof = Math.max((double)rad / 10.0, 1.0);
3694:         double rax = random.nextDouble() + 0.5;
3695:         double raz = 1.0 - rax + 0.5 + 0.5;
3696:         for (int i = 0; i <= rad; ++i) {
3697:             for (int j = 0; j <= rad / 2; ++j) {
3698:                 for (int e = 0; e <= rad; ++e) {
3699:                     int posx = xx + i - rad / 2;
3700:                     int posy = yy + j;
3701:                     int posz = zz + e - rad / 2;
3702:                     double dis = Math.sqrt(Math.pow(xx - posx, 2.0) * rax + Math.pow(yy - posy, 2.0) + Math.pow(zz - posz, 2.0) * raz);
3703:                     if (!(dis < (double)rad / 2.0 / (1.0 + Math.abs(rax - raz) / 3.0) + (random.nextDouble() * rad_cof - rad_cof / 2.0))) continue;
3704:                     world.func_147468_f(posx, posy, posz);
3705:                 }
3706:             }
3707:         }

4367:     private void generateHill(int xx, int yy, int zz, int rad, World world, Random random) {
4368:         int posz;
4369:         int posy;
4370:         int posx;
4371:         int e;
4372:         int j;

4375:         double rax = 1.0;
4376:         double raz = 1.0;

4761:                         continue;

4766:                 world.func_147465_d(firstBlockXCoord, 16, firstBlockZCoord, MFQM.MudBlock, 3, 0);

4768:                 world.func_147465_d(firstBlockXCoord, 14, firstBlockZCoord, MFQM.MudBlock, 3, 0);

4773:             boolean dep = false;
